28:["$","p",null,{"children":"Note: OBP will generally return a set of possibly non-commuting observables. QESEM automatically optimizes measurement bases when target observables contain non-commuting terms. It generates candidate measurement basis sets using several heuristic algorithms and selects the set that minimizes the number of distinct bases.
